Let's derive update for flipping a bit from 0 to1 (and other 1 to0) using current h; O(n) with numpy vector ops.

For current h, define for d=1..n-1 arrays of indicator differences:
R[d] = sum over i 0..n-d-1 (h_i & ~h_{i+d})
L[d] = sum (~h_i & h_{i+d})
When flipping bit at position p from 0 to1:
- It affects pairs where second endpoint p: i=p-d. For each d≤p: L[p-d] increases by 1 if h_{p-d}=0? Actually before flip h_p=0. Pair i=p-d, i+d=p. L counts h_i=0,h_p=1. Before h_p=0 so 0. After h_p=1, if h_i=0 then +1, if h_i=1 then 0. So L[d'] for d'=d (pair distance) += (1 - h_{p-d}). Also R? If h_i=1,h_p=1 no.
- Pairs where first endpoint p: i=p, p+d< n. R[p+d] counts h_p=1,h_{p+d}=0. Before 0. After +1 if h_{p+d}=0. L[p+d] before if h_p=0,h_{p+d}=1? If h_p flips to1, L at distance p+d decreases by 1 if h_{p+d}=1 (since pair 0->1 becomes same 1). So:
For d=1..n-p-1:
  R[p+d] += (1 - h_{p+d})
  L[p+d] -= h_{p+d}
Also need when d'? For first endpoint p, R for distance d where endpoint p is first? Yes R[d] pair (p,p+d). If h_p 0->1, R becomes 1 if right zero, L becomes 0 if right one. So update as above.
For second endpoint p, distance d where pair (p-d,p):
  L[d] += (1 - h_{p-d})
  R[d] +=? If h_{p-d}=1 and h_p=0, R before? R counts first one, second zero. Before h_p=0 and h_{p-d}=1 => R=1. After second one => R=0. So R[d] -= h_{p-d}.
Thus flipping p 0->1 updates:
for d=1..p:
  L[d-1] += 1 - h[p-d]
  R[d-1] -= h[p-d]
for d=1..n-p-1:
  R[d-1] += 1 - h[p+d]
  L[d-1] -= h[p+d]
This is O(n) vectorized with slices:
left_mask = 1-h[:p]; R[:p] -= h[:p]; L[:p] += 1-h[:p]
right_mask = h[p+1:]; R[p:p+n-p-1] += 1-h[p+1:]; L[p:p+n-p-1] -= h[p+1:]
Check indices: d=1..p => array index d-1 0..p-1. D=1 corresponds lag1. R length n-1: R[d-1]. Good. Right d=1..n-p-1 => index d-1=0..n-p-2, not p? Wait pair (p,p+d) distance d. So R[d-1] for d=1..n-p-1, index 0..n-p-2. That's not p. I mistakenly set R[p:p+...]. Need right-affected lags start at 1 independent of p. For all d, distance d. So:
R[:n-p-1] += 1 - h[p+1:]
L[:n-p-1] -= h[p+1:]
Left-affected distances d=1..p:
L[:p] += 1 - h[:p] (reversed? Pair (p-d,p) distance d: h[p-d] corresponds array index d-1 equals h[p-d]. So left vector should be h at positions p-1 down to 0: h_rev = h[:p][::-1]. Then L[:p] += 1 - h_rev; R[:p] -= h_rev. Because R index d-1 uses first endpoint p-d. For d=1, first p-1. So h[:p][::-1]. Similarly L.
Right affected: positions p+1.. use h_right = h[p+1:], d=1..; R[:n-p-1] += 1 - h_right; L[:n-p-1] -= h_right.
Flipping 1->0 is reverse: for left, p becomes0: before h_p=1. Pair (p-d,p): before L=0, R= h[p-d]. After second0: R decreases by h[p-d] (same), L increases by 1-h[p-d] (same). Interesting update same as 0->1? Wait if p 1->0, R[d] = h[p-d]? Before 1 (if left one), after 0, so R -=h_left. L before 0 if left zero? pair left0, p1 => L? L counts left0 right1 =1 if left zero. After p0 =>0, so L -= (1-h_left). That's different. Let's derive generally using indicator changes to avoid sign mistakes.
For each pair at distance d:
State bits a=left, b=right before. R = a(1-b); L=(1-a)b.
If flip right p from old to new (0->1 or1->0), with left bit a unchanged:
Delta L = (1-a)*new_b - (1-a)*old_b = (1-a)(new-old) = (1-a)Δb.
Delta R = a*(1-new_b) - a*(1-old_b) = -a Δb.
If flip left p from old to new, right b unchanged:
Delta L = (1-new_a)b - (1-old_a)b = -Δa b.
Delta R = new_a(1-b) - old_a(1-b)= Δa(1-b).
Where Δb = new-old. For 0->1 Δb=+1: right flip: L +=1-a, R -=a. Left flip: Δa=+1: R +=1-b, L -=b. For 1->0 Δb=-1: right flip: L -=1-a, R +=a. Left flip: Δa=-1: R -=1-b, L +=b.
So updating magnitude depends on old/new. For a 1<->0 swap (left i, right j) can do two flips sequentially: flip i 1->0 (using current h before flip) then flip j 0->1 (using h with i updated). But h changes at i and j; when flipping j, vectors include h_i updated? For pairs involving both i and j, sequential update may double-count? If we apply formula sequentially with updated h, then for pairs with endpoints i,j: first flip i updates pair (min,max) using old h_j; second flip j updates same pair using h_i new. Combined should yield correct. If doing vectorized sequentially on R/L arrays and after first flip but before updating h? Need use h values for pairs excluding the bit being flipped; if other bit not yet flipped, correct for first; for second, other bit already flipped, formula uses current h, correct. It should work if we update h after each flip. Complexity 2O(n) per swap.

def evaluate(h,n): returns c5 = np.max(np.corr?)*dx.

def smooth_value_grad(h,n,beta):
    oh=1-h
    c=np.correlate(h,oh,mode='full')  # length 2n-1
    g=c*dx
    # numeric safe
    gm=g.max()
    # If gm - second max large, weights concentrated. Use exp(beta*(g-gm))
    e=np.exp(beta*(g-gm))
    w=e/e.sum()
    f=gm + np.log(e.sum())/beta
    # gradient:
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    # For each offset idx 0..2n-2, o=idx-(n-1), weight w[idx]
    # If weight too small skip
    # Need efficient loop. Use direct offset vectorization.
    for idx, wo in enumerate(w):
       if wo < 1e-12 and beta large? But many small; skip maybe.
       o=idx-(n-1)
       if o>=0:
          q=0; p=o
          length=n-o
          # grad[p:p+length] += wo*(1-h[q:q+length])
          # grad[q:q+length] -= wo*h[p:p+length]
       else:
          p=0; q=-o; length=n+o
          # h[p:p+length]*(1-h[q:q+length])
    Multiply by dx? c was unscaled; g=c*dx. w from g. ∂g/∂h = dx*∂c. grad = dx * sum. If we use w from g, multiply grad by dx. Or in loop use wo and after grad*=dx.
    return f, grad, w, c, gm

We can also implement a custom `project_feasible` faster. For initial/resample. Use clipping and proportional scaling respecting bounds via active set (Dykstra?). Previous project loop 5000 O(n) okay. But can implement simple:
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          free=np.where(h<1-1e-15)[0]
          if free.size==0: break
          h[free]+=diff/len(free)
       else:
          free=np.where(h>1e-15)[0]
          if free.size==0: break
          h[free]+=diff/len(free)
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final adjust one point?
    diff=target-h.sum(); if abs(diff): find any with room.
This may not exactly hit target due active; can use previous proportional. It can loop many. Use a robust water-filling:
Given h clipped, diff. If need add, distribute among h<1 proportionally to remaining? Previous add diff*h for remove among h>0; for add diff*room. Then repeat. Good.

Resampling between n: Use linear interpolation then project. Also can use FFT resample. Use np.interp.

Let's design `slsqp_smooth` top-level:

def slsqp_smooth(h, n, beta, maxiter, deadline, relax_time=60):
    global _G_N, _G_BETA
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    _G_N=n; _G_BETA=beta
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_cons_sum,'jac':_cons_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    # SLSQP options: maxiter, ftol, maxls?
    options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0}
    try:
       res=minimize(_obj, h, jac=_jac, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, method='SLSQP', options=options)
       return project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except: return h

`make_binary_counts(h)`: h binary, compute R,L efficiently:
R=np.zeros(n-1); L=np.zeros(n-1)
for d in range(1,n):
   left=h[:-d]; right=h[d:]
   R[d-1]=np.sum(left & (1-right)) # for binary
   L[d-1]=np.sum((1-left) & right)
This loop n times O(n²) but n=250/512 okay. Could use FFT for speed, but initial few. Use numpy dot? For binary: R[d] = sum h[:-d] - sum h[:-d]*h[d:]; L similarly. def try_swap(i,j): # i one, j zero
   current_max = max(R.max(),L.max())
   flip i
   flip j
   new_max = max(R.max(),L.max())
   return new_max

Implement FFT convolution:
```
def _grad_from_weights(x, w, n):
    dx=2.0/n
    oh=1.0-x
    B=w.astype(float)
    D=w[::-1].astype(float)
    need=3*n-2
    N=1
    while N<need: N <<=1
    # use rfft
    FB=np.fft.rfft(B,N); FD=np.fft.rfft(D,N)
    Foh=np.fft.rfft(oh,N); Fx=np.fft.rfft(x,N)
    F=np.fft.irfft(Foh*FB,N)[n-1:2*n-1]
    P=np.fft.irfft(Fx*FD,N)[n-1:2*n-1]
    return dx*(F-P)
```
But computing rfft of B,D each jac; B/D length 2n-1. We can precompute N for n in SLSQP call and maybe use global `_G_NFFT`. Also `w` real. Good.
Potential numerical error in gradient due to floating; SLSQP may complain if gradient noisy. Convolution via FFT has small errors ~1e-12. Could direct for n<=200 to be exact? Maybe direct offset exact? Direct vectorized exact (sum products) but more code. FFT errors negligible. But SLSQP finite diff check? It trusts jac. Okay.

Now binary search more details:
`binary_counts_from_h(h)`: h binary int. Use dot loop.
```
def compute_binary_counts(hb,n):
    P=np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(hb)))
    R=np.empty(n-1,dtype=np.int64); L=...
    m=P[-1]
    for d in range(1,n):
       same=int(np.dot(hb[:-d], hb[d:]))
       ones_left=P[n-d]
       ones_right=m-P[d]
       R[d-1]=ones_left-same
       L[d-1]=ones_right-same
    return R,L
```
For binary 0/1 float, dot works. Need h int? Use `hb = (hb>0.5).astype(np.int64)`.

`max_count(R,L)` returns int.

Let's refine `project_feasible` for general with target possibly fractional.
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1-h
            s=room.sum()
            if s<1e-14: break
            h += diff*room/s
        else:
            s=h.sum()
            if s<1e-14: break
            h += diff*h/s
        h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # exact finish
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.argmin(1-h)
          add=min(diff,1-h[idx]); h[idx]+=add
       else:
          idx=np.argmax(h); reduce=min(-diff,h[idx]); h[idx]-=reduce
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
If target fractional and adding to one may not exact; loop already. Good.

To find improving swaps systematically, we can add a local greedy mode: while time, sample 200 random swaps; if any improves, accept and restart sampling. If no improvement in 20000, restart with random perturbations of current best. The anneal already.

Let's detail binary random function:
```
def random_binary_anneal(hb,n,deadline,start_T,seed,max_restarts_inside=3):
    rng=RandomState(seed)
    bestM=inf; best_h=None
    for restart in range(max_restarts_inside):
       if time>deadline-5: break
       if restart==0: start hb
       else: mutate best_h by swap 10 random pairs
       compute counts
       M=max
       ones/zeros lists
       T=start_T
       no_improve=0
       while time<deadline-2 and no_improve<50000:
          i=ones[rng.randrange(len(ones))]; j=zeros[...]
          oldM=M
          flip(i); flip(j)
          newM=int(max(R.max(),L.max()))
          if newM < oldM or (newM==oldM and rng.random()<0.2) or (newM>oldM and rng.random()<min(1.0, math.exp(-(newM-oldM)/T))):
             # accept
             M=newM
             update lists
             if M<bestM: bestM=M; best_h=hb.copy()
             no_improve=0 if newM<=oldM else no_improve+1
          else:
             # undo
             flip(j); flip(i)
             no_improve +=1
          if iter % 1000 ==0: T = max(0.05, T*0.98)
    return best_h,bestM
```
But no_improve should reset on improvement including newM<oldM. If accept equal, maybe not.
Use `np.max` returns int; if R/L int. Counts could become negative due bugs? Use int.

oz=zeros.index(j); zeros[oz]=zeros[-1]; zeros.pop()
ones.append(j); zeros.append(i)

def random_smooth_h(n,rng,maxfreq=0.3):
   spec=np.fft.rfft(rng.randn(n))
   spec[0]=0
   limit=max(1,int(maxfreq*n/2))
   spec[limit:]=0
   # random phase? spec = spec/(abs+eps)*rng...? not needed
   h=np.fft.irfft(spec,n)
   h-=h.mean()
   mx=np.max(np.abs(h))
   if mx>0: h*=0.5/mx
   h=0.5+h
   return project_feasible(h,n)
```

def resample_h(h,n_old,n_new):
   x_old=np.linspace(0,2,n_old,endpoint=False)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n_new,endpoint=False)
   hh=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)

Now code details for `_smooth_from_h(h,n,beta)` without global? We can implement standalone:
```
def smooth_value_and_weights(h,n,beta):
    oh=1.0-h
    c=np.correlate(h, oh, mode='full')
    g=c*(2.0/n)
    gm=g.max()
    e=np.exp(beta*(g-gm))
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return gm, np.full(2*n-1,1/(2*n-1)), gm
    f=gm+np.log(s)/beta
    w=e/s
    return f,w,gm
```
If beta very high and only one max, w concentrated; log(s) maybe 0. Good.
Gradient:
```
def smooth_grad_from_weights(h,n,w):
    dx=2.0/n
    h=np.asarray(h)
    oh=1.0-h
    # direct convolve
    B=w.astype(np.float64)
    D=w[::-1].astype(np.float64)
    if n <= 700:
       F=np.convolve(oh,B,mode='full')
       P=np.convolve(h,D,mode='full')
       F=F[n-1:2*n-1]; P=P[n-1:2*n-1]
    else:
       need=3*n-2; N=1<<(need-1).bit_length()
       F=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(oh,N)*np.fft.rfft(B,N),N)
       P=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h,N)*np.fft.rfft(D,N),N)
       F=F[n-1:2*n-1]; P=P[n-1:2*n-1]
    return dx*(F-P)
```
Check B/D dtypes. `np.convolve` length? oh length n, B length 2n-1. Output length 3n-2. Slice okay.
Test gradient for constant h=0.5: g all? For constant h, c_o = overlap_len*0.25? g_o. Weights maybe not symmetric? c symmetric? For h=0.5, c_o? h=oh=0.5, c_o=0.25*overlap length. Symmetric. Gradient of smooth max? Since h mean fixed? General gradient not zero because prefer increase? If symmetric weights, F/P maybe? For h constant and weights symmetric, grad = dx (∑W(0.5) -∑W_rev(0.5))=0 if W sym. If W not sym (objective max includes asymmetric shifts? But c for constant symmetric, weights sym.), gradient zero. Good.

SLSQP run:
```
def slsqp_builder_sequence(v, n_out, mode, alphas, maxiter, time_end):
    global _G_IN,...
    m = n_out if mode==0 else n_out//2
    best_v = _clip_project_v(v.copy(), mode, n_out)
    best_h = _build_h_v(best_v,n_out,mode)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h,n_out)
    _G_MODE=mode; _G_OUT=n_out; _G_IN=m
    constraints=[]
    if mode in (0,2): constraints=[...]
    bounds=[(0,1)]*m
    for beta in alphas:
       if time.time() > time_end - 15: break
       if beta > _G_BETA? set _G_BETA=beta; _invalidate cache
       # optional maxiter adjust: if beta > 1000, maxiter=max(50, maxiter//2)
       try:
          res=minimize(_obj,best_v,jac=_jac,bounds=bounds,constraints=constraints,method='SLSQP',options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-10,'iprint':0})
          v2=_clip_project_v(res.x,mode,n_out)
          c2=compute_c5(_build_h_v(v2,n_out,mode),n_out)
          if np.isfinite(c2) and c2<best_c:
              best_v=v2; best_c=c2
          # decide next start
          if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 <= best_c + 0.02:
              start_v=v2
          else:
              start_v=best_v
       except Exception:
          start_v=best_v
    best_h=_build_h_v(best_v,n_out,mode)
    return best_v,best_c,best_h
```
But if best_c updated, start_v maybe v2 even if not best? We set if c2 <= best_c+0.02; after update best_c=c2, okay. If not, start from best.

def to_complement_v(h,n):

Let's detail `smooth_value_and_weights`:
```
def smooth_value_and_weights(h,n,beta):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64)
    oh=1.0-h
    c=np.correlate(h, oh, mode='full')
    g=c*(2.0/n)
    gm=float(np.max(g))
    if not np.isfinite(gm): return gm,None,gm
    # if beta <=0 return max
    if beta <= 0:
       w=np.zeros(2*n-1); w[int(np.argmax(g))]=1.0; f=gm
    else:
       e=np.exp(beta*(g-gm))
       s=e.sum()
       if s<=0 or not finite: w=argmax; f=gm
       else: f=gm+np.log(s)/beta; w=e/s
    return f,w,gm
```
If beta small maybe. For β high and many g equal max, w uniform among max.

`smooth_grad_from_weights`: Use direct np.convolve; check sizes.
```
def smooth_grad_from_weights(h,n,w):
    dx=2.0/n
    h=np.asarray(h,float); oh=1.0-h; w=np.asarray(w,float)
    B=w; D=w[::-1]
    if n <= 800:
        fullF=np.convolve(oh, B, mode='full')
        fullP=np.convolve(h, D, mode='full')
    else:
        need=3*n-2
        N=1
        while N<need: N <<=1
        FB=np.fft.rfft(B,N); FD=np.fft.rfft(D,N)
        F=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(oh,N)*FB,N)
        P=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h,N)*FD,N)
        fullF=F; fullP=P
    return dx*(fullF[n-1:2*n-1] - fullP[n-1:2*n-1])
```
Potential issue: For n=1, 2n-1=1, slice [0:1]; ok. But n>1.

Now `_build_h_v`:
```
def _build_h_v(v,n_out,mode):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
    if mode==0:
        return v.copy()
    m=n_out//2
    if mode==1:
        h=np.empty(n_out,float); h[:m]=v; h[m:]=1.0-v; return h
    elif mode==2:
        h=np.empty(n_out,float); h[:m]=v; h[m:]=v; return h
    else: return v.copy()
```
If n_out odd and mode1? skip.

`_clip_project_v`:
```
def _clip_project_v(v,mode,n_out):
    v=np.asarray(v,float).copy()
    if mode==0:
       return project_feasible(v,n_out)
    if mode==1:
       return np.clip(v,0,1)
    if mode==2:
       m=n_out//2
       return project_feasible(v,m)
    return np.clip(v,0,1)
```

Cache functions:
```
def _invalidate_cache():
    global _LAST_V,_LAST_W,_LAST_H,_LAST_F
    _LAST_V=None; _LAST_W=None; _LAST_H=None; _LAST_F=0.0

def _cache_valid(v):
    return _LAST_V is not None and v.shape==_LAST_V.shape and np.array_equal(v,_LAST_V)
```
`_obj`:
```
def _obj(v):
    global _LAST_V,...
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): return 1e6
    if _cache_valid(v): return _LAST_F
    h=_build_h_v(v,_G_OUT,_G_MODE)
    f,w,gm=smooth_value_and_weights(h,_G_OUT,_G_BETA)
    if w is None or not np.isfinite(f): return 1e6
    _LAST_V=v.copy(); _LAST_F=f; _LAST_W=w; _LAST_H=h.copy()
    return f
```
`_jac`:
```
def _jac(v):
    _obj(v)
    if _LAST_W is None: return np.ones(v.shape)
    grad_h=smooth_grad_from_weights(_LAST_H,_G_OUT,_LAST_W)
    if _G_MODE==0: grad=grad_h
    elif _G_MODE==1: grad=grad_h[:_G_IN]-grad_h[_G_IN:]
    elif _G_MODE==2: grad=grad_h[:_G_IN]+grad_h[_G_IN:]
    else: grad=grad_h
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad=np.zeros_like(v)
    return grad
```
Need ensure after `_obj`, cache valid for same v. If `_obj` returned 1e6 due nonfinite and set cache? If nonfinite, no. If f nonfinite, return 1e6 but not set cache? We check before set. If w None, _LAST maybe previous? In _obj, if invalid, should invalidate? Better:
```
if not finite: _invalidate_cache(); return 1e6
```
In `_jac`, call `_obj`, if `_LAST_V` is not this v? But if _obj invalidated, _LAST_V None; return zeros. Use `_cache_valid(v)`.

    return float(np.sum(v)-target)

`slsqp_builder_sequence`:
```
def slsqp_builder_sequence(v, n_out, mode, alphas, maxiter, time_end):
    global _G_IN,_G_OUT,_G_MODE,_G_BETA
    if n_out < 2: return ...
    if mode in (1,2) and n_out %2 !=0: mode=0? For safety return current.
    m = n_out if mode==0 else n_out//2
    v=_clip_project_v(v.copy(),mode,n_out)
    best_c=compute_c5(_build_h_v(v,n_out,mode),n_out)
    best_v=v.copy()
    _G_MODE=mode; _G_OUT=n_out; _G_IN=m
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
    cons=[]
    if mode in (0,2):
       cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sum_func,'jac':_sum_jac}]
    for beta in alphas:
       if time.time() > time_end - 10: break
       _G_BETA=float(beta); _invalidate_cache()
       base=maxiter
       if beta > 2000: base=max(50, maxiter//2)
       else: base=maxiter
       try:
          res=minimize(_obj,best_v,jac=_jac,bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,method='SLSQP',
                       options={'maxiter':int(base),'ftol':1e-10,'iprint':0})
          v2=_clip_project_v(res.x,mode,n_out)
          h2=_build_h_v(v2,n_out,mode)
          c2=compute_c5(h2,n_out)
       except Exception:
          v2=best_v.copy(); c2=best_c
       if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
          best_v=v2.copy(); best_c=c2
       if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 <= best_c + 0.02:
          start_v=v2.copy()
       else:
          start_v=best_v.copy()
    best_h=_build_h_v(best_v,n_out,mode)
    return best_v,best_c,best_h
```
If mode=1, best_c maybe from h with sum not exactly due floating; compute_c5 fine. For final project later.

`compute_c5` with h shape maybe floating not sum exact; objective unaffected except maybe correlations; sum constraint not in compute. Good.

Binary functions:
```
def initial_binary(h,n):
   m=n//2
   idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:m]
   hb=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int64); hb[idx]=1; return hb
```
`compute_binary_counts`:
```
def compute_binary_counts(hb,n):
   hb=np.asarray(hb)
   P=np.concatenate(([0],np.cumsum(hb)))
   m=P[-1]
   R=np.zeros(n-1,dtype=np.int64); L=np.zeros(n-1,dtype=np.int64)
   for d in range(1,n):
      same=int(np.dot(hb[:-d],hb[d:]))
      R[d-1]=P[n-d]-same
      L[d-1]=(m-P[d])-same
   return R,L
```
Check ones_right = total ones - ones up to d (positions <d). For pair (p,p+d), right positions d..n-1. Sum ones right = m - P[d]. Good.

`flip_bit_update` as above. Use `h[p]` old. Need ensure h dtype int64, R/L int64.
```
def flip_bit_update(h,R,L,p):
    n=len(h)
    old=h[p]
    if old == 1:
       if p>0:
          left=h[:p][::-1]
          R[:p] += left
          L[:p] -= (1-left)
       if p<n-1:
          right=h[p+1:]
          R[:n-p-1] -= (1-right)
          L[:n-p-1] += right
       h[p]=0
    else:
       if p>0:
          left=h[:p][::-1]
          R[:p] -= left
          L[:p] += (1-left)
       if p<n-1:
          right=h[p+1:]
          R[:n-p-1] += (1-right)
          L[:n-p-1] -= right
       h[p]=1
    return h[p]
```
Since R,L int, `1-left` int. Good.

`random_binary_anneal`:
Need choose start_T. Count differences. If C target ~ M=47 at n=250; start_T=5 maybe too low; use 8. MinT=0.2.
```
def random_binary_anneal(hb,n,deadline,seed,start_T=8.0):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    bestM=10**9; best_h=None
    time_left = deadline - time.time()
    for restart in range(4):
       if time.time()>deadline-3 or time_left<=0: break
       if restart==0:
          cur=hb.copy()
       else:
          if best_h is None: break
          cur=best_h.copy()
          # perturb 10-30 random swaps
          ones=np.where(cur==1)[0].tolist(); zeros=np.where(cur==0)[0].tolist()
          for _ in range(rng.randint(10,40)):
             i=rng.choice(ones); j=rng.choice(zeros); cur[i],cur[j]=0,1
          recompute (not R/L yet)
       R,L=compute_binary_counts(cur,n); M=max(...)
       if M<bestM: bestM=M; best_h=cur.copy()
       ones_list=list(map(int,np.where(cur==1)[0])); zeros_list=...
       T=start_T
       no_improve=0
       # time for restart proportional
       while time.time()<deadline-2 and no_improve<30000:
          if not ones_list or not zeros_list: break
          i=ones_list[rng.randrange(len(ones_list))]; j=zeros_list[rng.randrange(len(zeros_list))]
          oldM=M
          flip_bit_update(cur,R,L,i); flip_bit_update(cur,R,L,j)
          newM=int(max(R.max(),L.max()))
          # accept criteria
          acc=False
          if newM < oldM: acc=True
          elif newM == oldM and rng.random()<0.1: acc=True
          elif newM > oldM:
             d=newM-oldM
             if d <= 5 and rng.random() < math.exp(-float(d)/T): acc=True
          if acc:
             M=newM
             # update lists
             pos=ones_list.index(i); ones_list[pos]=ones_list[-1]; ones_list.pop()
             pos=zeros_list.index(j); zeros_list[pos]=zeros_list[-1]; zeros_list.pop()
             ones_list.append(j); zeros_list.append(i)
             if newM < bestM:
                 bestM=newM; best_h=cur.copy()
             no_improve=0
          else:
             flip_bit_update(cur,R,L,j); flip_bit_update(cur,R,L,i)
             no_improve+=1
          # cool every 5000
          if random_count? track iter
             T=max(0.2,T*0.99)
    return best_h,bestM
```
Need track iter for cooling. Use `it=0`; each iteration `it +=1`; if it%1000==0: T=max...`.
Reset no_improve only on strict improvement maybe; equal not. Use if newM<oldM: no_improve=0 else no_improve +=1? But if many equals, might restart. Better if newM<=oldM: no_improve=0.

Random smooth h:
```
def random_smooth_h(n,rng,maxfreq=0.25):
    spec=np.fft.rfft(rng.randn(n), n=n)
    spec[0]=0
    lim=max(1,int(maxfreq*n/2))
    if len(spec)>lim: spec[lim:]=0
    h=np.fft.irfft(spec,n=n)
    h-=h.mean()
    mx=np.max(np.abs(h))
    if mx>0: h *= 0.5/mx
    h = 0.5 + h
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
`random_mix_h`:
```
def random_mix_h(base,n,seed,alpha):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    r=random_smooth_h(n,rng,maxfreq=0.2+rng.random()*0.3)
    h=(1-alpha)*base + alpha*r
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

def random_complement_v(n, base_v, seed, alpha):

Detailed code for `random_smooth_h`:
```
def random_smooth_h(n, rng, maxfreq=0.25):
    spec = np.fft.rfft(np.random.randn(n), n=n)
    spec[0]=0.0
    lim = max(1, int(maxfreq * n // 2))
    if len(spec) > lim:
        spec[lim:] = 0.0
    h = np.fft.irfft(spec, n=n)
    h -= h.mean()
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
    if mx > 1e-12:
        h *= 0.5 / mx
    else:
        h = rng.randn(n); h -= h.mean(); mx=...; h*=0.5/mx
    h = 0.5 + h
    return np.clip(h,0,1)

```
def fourier_complement_make(P,n):
   m=n//2
   t=np.linspace(0,1,m,endpoint=False)
   v=0.5*np.ones(m)
   # use frequencies 1..len(P)//2
   K=len(P)//2
   for k in range(K):
       a=P[2*k]; b=P[2*k+1]
       w=(k+1)*np.pi*? If using [0,1], cos(2π(k+1)t)
       v += a*np.cos(2*np.pi*(k+1)*t)+b*np.sin(...)
   v = v - v.mean() + 0.5
   mx=np.max(np.abs(v-0.5))
   if mx>0.5: v=0.5+(v-0.5)*0.5/mx
   v=np.clip(v,0,1)
   h=np.empty(n); h[:m]=v; h[m:]=1-v
   return h
def fourier_complement_obj(P,n):
   h=fourier_complement_make(P,n); return compute_c5(h,n)
```

Old=0 right: R += (1-right); L -= right.
Let's rederive from general formulas to ensure no sign swapped:
Old=1 -> new0, Δ=-1.
As right endpoint (p-d,p): Δb=-1. ΔL=(1-a)Δ=-(1-a); ΔR=-aΔ=+a. Code old=1 left: L -=1-left; R +=left. correct.
As left endpoint (p,p+d): Δa=-1. ΔR=(1-b)Δ=-(1-b); ΔL=-bΔ=+b. Code old=1 right: R -=1-right; L +=right. correct.
Old=0 -> new1, Δ=+1.
As right: L +=1-a; R -=a. Code old=0 left: R -=left; L +=1-left. correct.
As left: R +=1-b; L -=b. Code old=0 right: R +=1-right; L -=right. correct.
Good.

def best_binary_search(h_init,n,deadline,seed):
    if n%2!=0 or n<16 or time.time()>deadline-20: return None, np.inf
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    m=n//2
    cand0=initial_binary(h_init,n)
    bestM=np.inf; best_h=None
    # Time shares
    t_start=time.time(); total=deadline-t_start
    starts=[cand0]
    for s in range(3):
       b=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int64); b[rng.choice(n,m,replace=False)]=1; starts.append(b)
    # maybe one start from sorted? e.g., block? not.
    # First start gets 50% time, others 16%
    for idx,cand in enumerate(starts):
       if time.time()>deadline-10: break
       if idx==0: share=0.5*total
       elif idx==1: share=0.2*total
       else: share=0.1*total
       # if cand is best from previous? For later, maybe start from best_h if found else random
       if best_h is not None and idx>1:
           cand=best_h.copy()
       end=min(deadline-5, time.time()+max(10,share))
       hb,M=random_binary_anneal(cand,n,end,seed+idx*13)
       if M<bestM: bestM=M; best_h=hb.copy()
    # If time left, polish best_h for remaining with no random increases (strict)
    if best_h is not None and time.time()<deadline-10:
       R,L=compute_binary_counts(best_h,n); M=int(max(R.max(),L.max()))
       ones=... zeros=...; rng2=RandomState(seed+999); T=0.2
       while time.time()<deadline-3:
          for attempt in range(20):
             i=rng2.choice(ones); j=rng2.choice(zeros)
             oldM=M; flip; newM; if newM<oldM accept update; else undo
          if M<bestM: bestM=M; best_h=best_h.copy()
          if no improvement for 10000? break maybe
    return best_h,bestM
```

```
def fourier_complement_make(P,n):
    m=n//2
    if m<=0: raise? return zeros
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m,endpoint=False)
    v=0.5*np.ones(m)
    K=len(P)//2
    for k in range(K):
       a=float(P[2*k]); b=float(P[2*k+1])
       w=2*np.pi*(k+1)*t
       v += a*np.cos(w)+b*np.sin(w)
    v = v - v.mean() + 0.5
    dev=v-0.5
    mx=float(np.max(np.abs(dev)))
    if mx > 0.5:
       v=0.5 + dev*(0.5/mx)
    v=np.clip(v,0,1)
    h=np.empty(n); h[:m]=v; h[m:]=1-v
    return h
def fourier_complement_obj(P,n):
    try:
       h=fourier_complement_make(P,n); return compute_c5(h,n)
    except: return 1e6
```
Could use frequencies 1,2,3,4. Since v on [0,1], sin/cos zero mean with endpoint false. Good.
K=4 maybe. Could use K=5 10 params. Nelder-Mead with 10 dims 400. Use K=4 for speed.

```
def make_final_feasible(h,n):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    target=n/2.0
    diff=target-float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       if diff>0:
          cand=np.where(h<1.0)[0]
          if cand.size:
             idx=int(cand[np.argmin(1-h[cand])])
             add=min(diff,1-h[idx]); h[idx]+=add
       else:
          cand=np.where(h>0.0)[0]
          if cand.size:
             idx=int(cand[np.argmax(h[cand])])
             red=min(-diff,h[idx]); h[idx]-=red
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # if still off, adjust another?
    return h
```
This may not exactly fix if diff large; project did. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import math
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global state used by SLSQP callback functions.  These are not
# closures/lambdas; they are plain module-level mutable globals.
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
_G_IN = 0
_G_OUT = 0
_G_MODE = 0
_G_BETA = 1.0

_LAST_V = None
_LAST_H = None
_LAST_W = None
_LAST_F = 0.0


def compute_c5(h, n):
    """Exact task objective on the given grid."""
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    """Project to 0 <= h <= 1 and sum(h) = n/2."""
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(300):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            sr = float(np.sum(room))
            if sr < 1e-14:
                break
            h = h + (diff / sr) * room
        else:
            sh = float(np.sum(h))
            if sh < 1e-14:
                break
            h = h + (diff / sh) * h
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    # Final small correction so the sum is as exact as practical.
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            cand = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
            if cand.size:
                idx = int(cand[np.argmin(1.0 - h[cand])])
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
                h[idx] += add
        else:
            cand = np.where(h > 0.0)[0]
            if cand.size:
                idx = int(cand[np.argmax(h[cand])])
                sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
                h[idx] -= sub
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def smooth_value_and_weights(h, n, beta):
    """
    Return (log-sum-exp smooth max, softmax weights) for the lag values
        g = correlate(h, 1-h, full) * (2/n)
    The weights are aligned with np.correlate(..., 'full') indices.
    """
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    oh = 1.0 - h
    c = np.correlate(h, oh, mode="full")
    g = c * (2.0 / n)
    gm = float(np.max(g))
    if not np.isfinite(gm):
        return gm, None, gm

    if beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(g))] = 1.0
        return gm, w, gm

    e = np.exp(beta * (g - gm))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(g))] = 1.0
        return gm, w, gm

    f = gm + math.log(s) / float(beta)
    w = e / s
    return f, w, gm


def smooth_grad_from_weights(h, n, w):
    """
    Gradient of the log-sum-exp smoothed maximum.

    For offset o, the lag value is
        g_o = sum_q h_{q+o} (1 - h_q)
    and np.correlate(h,1-h,'full') is ordered as o = idx-(n-1).
    """
    dx = 2.0 / n
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float)
    oh = 1.0 - h

    B = w
    D = w[::-1]

    if n <= 800:
        Ffull = np.convolve(oh, B, mode="full")
        Pfull = np.convolve(h, D, mode="full")
    else:
        need = 3 * n - 2
        N = 1
        while N < need:
            N <<= 1
        FB = np.fft.rfft(B, N)
        FD = np.fft.rfft(D, N)
        Ffull = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(oh, N) * FB, N)
        Pfull = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h, N) * FD, N)

    return dx * (Ffull[n - 1:2 * n - 1] - Pfull[n - 1:2 * n - 1])


def _invalidate_cache():
    global _LAST_V, _LAST_H, _LAST_W, _LAST_F
    _LAST_V = None
    _LAST_H = None
    _LAST_W = None
    _LAST_F = 0.0


def _cache_valid(v):
    return (
        _LAST_V is not None
        and _LAST_W is not None
        and _LAST_H is not None
        and (v.shape == _LAST_V.shape)
        and bool(np.array_equal(v, _LAST_V))
    )


def _build_h_v(v, n_out, mode):
    """
    Build full h from reduced variables v.

    mode 0: general, h = v                (length n_out, sum constraint)
    mode 1: complement, h=[v, 1-v]        (length 2m, no sum constraint)
    mode 2: periodic,   h=[v, v]          (length 2m, sum constraint)
    """
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    if mode == 0:
        return v.copy()
    m = n_out // 2
    if mode == 1:
        h = np.empty(n_out, dtype=float)
        h[:m] = v
        h[m:] = 1.0 - v
        return h
    if mode == 2:
        h = np.empty(n_out, dtype=float)
        h[:m] = v
        h[m:] = v
        return h
    return v.copy()


def _clip_project_v(v, mode, n_out):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).copy()
    if mode == 0:
        return project_feasible(v, n_out)
    if mode == 1:
        return np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    if mode == 2:
        m = n_out // 2
        return project_feasible(v, m)
    return np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)


def _obj(v):
    global _LAST_V, _LAST_H, _LAST_W, _LAST_F
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        _invalidate_cache()
        return 1e6

    if _cache_valid(v):
        return _LAST_F

    h = _build_h_v(v, _G_OUT, _G_MODE)
    f, w, gm = smooth_value_and_weights(h, _G_OUT, _G_BETA)

    if w is None or not np.isfinite(f):
        _invalidate_cache()
        return 1e6

    _LAST_V = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).copy()
    _LAST_H = h.copy()
    _LAST_W = w.copy()
    _LAST_F = float(f)
    return float(f)


def _jac(v):
    _obj(v)
    if _LAST_W is None or _LAST_H is None:
        return np.zeros(v.shape, dtype=float)

    grad_h = smooth_grad_from_weights(_LAST_H, _G_OUT, _LAST_W)

    if _G_MODE == 0:
        grad = grad_h
    elif _G_MODE == 1:
        grad = grad_h[:_G_IN] - grad_h[_G_IN:]
    elif _G_MODE == 2:
        grad = grad_h[:_G_IN] + grad_h[_G_IN:]
    else:
        grad = grad_h

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(v.shape, dtype=float)
    return grad


def _sum_func(v):
    if _G_MODE == 0:
        target = 0.5 * _G_OUT
    elif _G_MODE == 2:
        target = 0.25 * _G_OUT
    else:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.sum(v) - target)


def _sum_jac(v):
    return np.ones(_G_IN, dtype=float)


def slsqp_builder_sequence(v, n_out, mode, alphas, maxiter, time_end):
    """
    Annealed SLSQP polishing of a parameterized family.

    Returns (best_v, best_c, best_h).
    """
    global _G_IN, _G_OUT, _G_MODE, _G_BETA

    if n_out < 4:
        h = _build_h_v(v, n_out, mode)
        return v, compute_c5(h, n_out), h.copy()

    if mode in (1, 2) and (n_out % 2 != 0):
        mode = 0
        n_out = len(v)

    m = n_out if mode == 0 else (n_out // 2)
    v = _clip_project_v(v.copy(), mode, n_out)
    best_v = v.copy()
    best_h = _build_h_v(best_v, n_out, mode)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n_out)

    _G_MODE = mode
    _G_OUT = n_out
    _G_IN = m

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
    constraints = []
    if mode in (0, 2):
        constraints = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _sum_func, "jac": _sum_jac}]

    for beta in alphas:
        if time.time() > time_end - 12:
            break
        _G_BETA = float(beta)
        _invalidate_cache()

        cur_maxiter = int(maxiter)
        if beta > 2500.0:
            cur_maxiter = max(50, cur_maxiter // 2)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _obj,
                best_v,
                jac=_jac,
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=constraints,
                method="SLSQP",
                options={"maxiter": cur_maxiter, "ftol": 1e-10, "iprint": 0},
            )
            if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
                v2 = _clip_project_v(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), mode, n_out)
                h2 = _build_h_v(v2, n_out, mode)
                c2 = compute_c5(h2, n_out)
            else:
                v2 = best_v.copy()
                c2 = best_c
        except Exception:
            v2 = best_v.copy()
            c2 = best_c

        if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
            best_v = v2.copy()
            best_c = float(c2)

        if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 <= best_c + 0.02:
            start_v = v2.copy()
        else:
            start_v = best_v.copy()
        best_v_for_next = start_v
        best_v = start_v

    best_h = _build_h_v(best_v, n_out, mode)
    return best_v, float(best_c), best_h


def resample_h(h, n_old, n_new):
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    hh = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=0.0, right=0.0)
    return project_feasible(hh, n_new)


def to_complement_v(h, n):
    """Project a full h onto the complement family h=[v,1-v]."""
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return None
    m = n // 2
    v = 0.5 * (h[:m] + (1.0 - h[m:2 * m]))
    return np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# Binary local search.
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
def initial_binary(h, n):
    m = n // 2
    idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1][:m]
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int64)
    hb[idx] = 1
    return hb


def compute_binary_counts(hb, n):
    """
    For binary hb:
      R[d] = #{i: h_i=1, h_{i+d}=0}
      L[d] = #{i: h_i=0, h_{i+d}=1}
    d=1..n-1 stored at index d-1.
    """
    hb = np.asarray(hb, dtype=np.int64)
    P = np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(hb, dtype=np.int64)))
    total_ones = int(P[-1])
    R = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.int64)
    L = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.int64)
    for d in range(1, n):
        same = int(np.dot(hb[:-d], hb[d:]))
        ones_left = int(P[n - d])
        ones_right = total_ones - int(P[d])
        R[d - 1] = ones_left - same
        L[d - 1] = ones_right - same
    return R, L


def flip_bit_update(h, R, L, p):
    """
    Flip binary h[p] and update R,L exactly.

    h: int array (modified in place)
    R,L: int arrays (modified in place)
    """
    n = len(h)
    old = int(h[p])
    if old == 1:
        if p > 0:
            left = h[:p][::-1]
            R[:p] += left
            L[:p] -= (1 - left)
        if p + 1 < n:
            right = h[p + 1:]
            R[:n - p - 1] -= (1 - right)
            L[:n - p - 1] += right
        h[p] = 0
    else:
        if p > 0:
            left = h[:p][::-1]
            R[:p] -= left
            L[:p] += (1 - left)
        if p + 1 < n:
            right = h[p + 1:]
            R[:n - p - 1] += (1 - right)
            L[:n - p - 1] -= right
        h[p] = 1
    return int(h[p])


def random_binary_anneal(hb, n, deadline, seed, start_T=8.0):
    """
    Randomized hill climbing / simulated annealing for binary sequences.
    Returns (best_binary_array, best_max_count).
    """
    hb = np.asarray(hb, dtype=np.int64).copy()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    bestM = 10 ** 9
    best_hb = None

    for restart in range(4):
        if time.time() > deadline - 3:
            break

        if restart == 0:
            cur = hb.copy()
        else:
            if best_hb is None:
                break
            cur = best_hb.copy()
            ones = np.where(cur == 1)[0]
            zeros = np.where(cur == 0)[0]
            for _ in range(int(rng.randint(8, 25))):
                if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
                    break
                i = int(rng.choice(ones))
                j = int(rng.choice(zeros))
                cur[i], cur[j] = 0, 1

        R, L = compute_binary_counts(cur, n)
        M = int(max(R.max(), L.max()))
        if M < bestM:
            bestM = M
            best_hb = cur.copy()

        ones_list = [int(x) for x in np.where(cur == 1)[0]]
        zeros_list = [int(x) for x in np.where(cur == 0)[0]]

        T = float(start_T)
        no_improve = 0
        it = 0

        while time.time() < deadline - 2 and no_improve < 30000:
            if not ones_list or not zeros_list:
                break

            i = ones_list[rng.randrange(len(ones_list))]
            j = zeros_list[rng.randrange(len(zeros_list))]
            oldM = M

            flip_bit_update(cur, R, L, i)
            flip_bit_update(cur, R, L, j)
            newM = int(max(R.max(), L.max()))

            accept = False
            if newM < oldM:
                accept = True
            elif newM == oldM and rng.random() < 0.10:
                accept = True
            elif newM > oldM:
                dd = newM - oldM
                if dd <= 5:
                    try:
                        acc_p = math.exp(-float(dd) / max(T, 1e-6))
                    except Exception:
                        acc_p = 0.0
                    if acc_p > 0.0 and rng.random() < acc_p:
                        accept = True

            if accept:
                M = newM
                # swap lists
                pos = ones_list.index(i)
                ones_list[pos] = ones_list[-1]
                ones_list.pop()
                zeros_list.append(i)

                pos = zeros_list.index(j)
                zeros_list[pos] = zeros_list[-1]
                zeros_list.pop()
                ones_list.append(j)

                if M < bestM:
                    bestM = M
                    best_hb = cur.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                # Undo: flip j back, then i back.
                flip_bit_update(cur, R, L, j)
                flip_bit_update(cur, R, L, i)
                no_improve += 1

            it += 1
            if it % 1500 == 0:
                T = max(0.15, T * 0.985)
                # Periodic recomputation guards against any accidental drift.
                R, L = compute_binary_counts(cur, n)
                M = int(max(R.max(), L.max()))
                if M < bestM:
                    bestM = M
                    best_hb = cur.copy()

    return best_hb, int(bestM)


def best_binary_search(h_init, n, deadline, seed):
    if n % 2 != 0 or n < 32 or time.time() > deadline - 25:
        return None, np.inf

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    m = n // 2
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(5.0, deadline - t0)

    cand0 = initial_binary(h_init, n)
    starts = [cand0]

    for _ in range(3):
        b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int64)
        b[rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)] = 1
        starts.append(b)

    bestM = np.inf
    best_hb = None

    for idx, cand in enumerate(starts):
        if time.time() > deadline - 10:
            break

        if idx == 0:
            share = 0.50 * total
        elif idx == 1:
            share = 0.20 * total
        else:
            share = 0.10 * total

        if idx > 1 and best_hb is not None:
            cand = best_hb.copy()

        end = min(deadline - 5.0, time.time() + max(8.0, share * 0.9))
        hb, M = random_binary_anneal(cand, n, end, seed + 101 * idx + 17)
        if M < bestM:
            bestM = M
            best_hb = hb.copy()

    # Strict final polish if there is time.
    if best_hb is not None and time.time() < deadline - 12:
        cur = best_hb.copy()
        R, L = compute_binary_counts(cur, n)
        M = int(max(R.max(), L.max()))
        ones_list = [int(x) for x in np.where(cur == 1)[0]]
        zeros_list = [int(x) for x in np.where(cur == 0)[0]]
        rng2 = np.random.RandomState(seed + 9999)
        stall = 0

        while time.time() < deadline - 4 and stall < 6000:
            improved = False
            for _ in range(20):
                if not ones_list or not zeros_list:
                    break
                i = ones_list[rng2.randrange(len(ones_list))]
                j = zeros_list[rng2.randrange(len(zeros_list))]
                oldM = M
                flip_bit_update(cur, R, L, i)
                flip_bit_update(cur, R, L, j)
                newM = int(max(R.max(), L.max()))
                if newM < oldM:
                    M = newM
                    pos = ones_list.index(i)
                    ones_list[pos] = ones_list[-1]
                    ones_list.pop()
                    zeros_list.append(i)

                    pos = zeros_list.index(j)
                    zeros_list[pos] = zeros_list[-1]
                    zeros_list.pop()
                    ones_list.append(j)
                    improved = True
                    if M < bestM:
                        bestM = M
                        best_hb = cur.copy()
                else:
                    flip_bit_update(cur, R, L, j)
                    flip_bit_update(cur, R, L, i)

            if improved:
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 20
                if stall >= 1200:
                    break

    return best_hb, int(bestM)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# Low-dimensional Fourier construction in the complement family.
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
def fourier_complement_make(P, n):
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return None
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, endpoint=False)
    v = 0.5 * np.ones(m, dtype=float)
    K = len(P) // 2
    for k in range(K):
        a = float(P[2 * k])
        b = float(P[2 * k + 1])
        w = 2.0 * np.pi * (k + 1) * t
        v += a * np.cos(w) + b * np.sin(w)

    v = v - float(np.mean(v)) + 0.5
    dev = v - 0.5
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(dev)))
    if mx > 0.5:
        v = 0.5 + dev * (0.5 / mx)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)

    h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    h[:m] = v
    h[m:] = 1.0 - v
    return h


def fourier_complement_obj(P, n):
    try:
        h = fourier_complement_make(P, n)
        if h is None:
            return 1e6
        return compute_c5(h, n)
    except Exception:
        return 1e6


def fourier_complement_search(n, seed, time_end, maxiter=300):
    """
    Try a few low-dimensional Fourier coefficients in the complement family.
    """
    if n % 2 != 0 or time.time() > time_end - 30:
        return None, np.inf

    best_h = None
    best_c = np.inf
    K = 4  # 4 cosine/sine pairs = 8 parameters

    for s in range(2):
        if time.time() > time_end - 20:
            break
        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + 7000 + s)
        x0 = rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.35, size=2 * K)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                fourier_complement_obj,
                x0,
                args=(n,),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "xatol": 1e-6, "fatol": 1e-7},
            )
            h = fourier_complement_make(res.x, n)
            if h is None:
                continue
            c = compute_c5(h, n)
            if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h, float(best_c)


def random_smooth_h(n, rng, maxfreq=0.25):
    spec = np.fft.rfft(rng.randn(n), n=n)
    spec[0] = 0.0
    lim = max(1, int(maxfreq * n // 2))
    if len(spec) > lim:
        spec[lim:] = 0.0
    h = np.fft.irfft(spec, n=n)
    h -= float(np.mean(h))
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
    if mx > 1e-12:
        h *= 0.5 / mx
    else:
        h = rng.randn(n)
        h -= float(np.mean(h))
        mx = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
        if mx > 1e-12:
            h *= 0.5 / mx
        else:
            h = np.zeros(n)
    h = 0.5 + h
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def random_mix_h(base, n, seed, alpha):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    maxfreq = float(0.15 + 0.30 * rng.random())
    r = random_smooth_h(n, rng, maxfreq)
    h = (1.0 - alpha) * base + alpha * r
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def choose_base_n(parent_n):
    if parent_n < 16:
        return 256
    if parent_n % 2 == 1:
        return parent_n
    if 240 <= parent_n <= 280:
        return 256
    if parent_n < 128:
        return 256
    if parent_n <= 512:
        return parent_n
    return parent_n


def main_run(seed=42, budget_s=1100):
    t0 = time.time()
    t_end = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    t_budget = float(budget_s) - 5.0

    # Load parent if present.
    try:
        parent_h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float, copy=True)
        parent_n = int(parent_h.size)
    except Exception:
        parent_n = 250
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, parent_n, endpoint=False)
        parent_h = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
        parent_h = project_feasible(parent_h, parent_n)

    if parent_n <= 0:
        parent_n = 250
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, parent_n, endpoint=False)
        parent_h = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
        parent_h = project_feasible(parent_h, parent_n)

    parent_h = project_feasible(parent_h, parent_n)
    parent_c = compute_c5(parent_h, parent_n)

    # Prefer n=256 if parent is around 250; binary thresholds are more favorable.
    base_n = choose_base_n(parent_n)
    if base_n != parent_n:
        base_h = resample_h(parent_h, parent_n, base_n)
        base_c = compute_c5(base_h, base_n)
    else:
        base_h = parent_h.copy()
        base_c = parent_c

    if base_c <= parent_c:
        best_h = base_h.copy()
        best_c = float(base_c)
        best_n = int(base_n)
    else:
        best_h = parent_h.copy()
        best_c = float(parent_c)
        best_n = int(parent_n)

    def update_with_h(h, n):
        nonlocal best_h, best_c, best_n
        try:
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            c = compute_c5(h, n)
            if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c:
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_n = int(n)
                best_c = float(c)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ---------------- Phase 1: complement SLSQP at base resolution -------
    p1 = t0 + 0.10 * t_budget
    if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < p1 - 15:
        v1 = to_complement_v(best_h, best_n)
        if v1 is not None:
            try:
                _, c1, h1 = slsqp_builder_sequence(
                    v1, best_n, 1,
                    alphas=[20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0],
                    maxiter=160,
                    time_end=p1
                )
                if np.isfinite(c1) and c1 < best_c:
                    best_c = float(c1)
                    best_h = h1.copy()
                    best_n = best_n
            except Exception:
                pass

    # ---------------- Phase 2: general SLSQP at base resolution ----------
    p2 = t0 + 0.30 * t_budget
    if time.time() < p2 - 15:
        try:
            _, c2, h2 = slsqp_builder_sequence(
                best_h.copy(), best_n, 0,
                alphas=[10.0, 50.0, 200.0, 1000.0, 4000.0],
                maxiter=160,
                time_end=p2
            )
            if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
                best_c = float(c2)
                best_h = h2.copy()
                best_n = best_n
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ---------------- Phase 3: binary local search at base resolution ----
    p3 = t0 + 0.50 * t_budget
    if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < p3 - 30:
        try:
            hb, Mb = best_binary_search(best_h, best_n, p3, seed + 11)
            if hb is not None and np.isfinite(Mb):
                cbin = float(Mb) * (2.0 / best_n)
                if cbin < best_c:
                    best_h = hb.astype(float).copy()
                    best_c = cbin
                    best_n = int(best_n)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ---------------- Phase 4: Fourier / random restarts -----------------
    p4 = t0 + 0.64 * t_budget
    if time.time() < p4 - 25:
        if best_n % 2 == 0:
            try:
                hf, cf = fourier_complement_search(best_n, seed + 123, p4 - 45, maxiter=220)
                if hf is not None and np.isfinite(cf) and cf < best_c:
                    best_h = hf.copy()
                    best_c = float(cf)
                    best_n = int(best_n)
            except Exception:
                pass

        for r in range(2):
            if time.time() > p4 - 25:
                break
            try:
                hrand = random_mix_h(best_h, best_n, seed + 2000 + 37 * r, alpha=0.55 + 0.18 * r)
                _, cr, hr = slsqp_builder_sequence(
                    hrand, best_n, 0,
                    alphas=[50.0, 500.0, 2000.0],
                    maxiter=90,
                    time_end=min(p4 - 5, time.time() + 40.0)
                )
                if np.isfinite(cr) and cr < best_c:
                    best_c = float(cr)
                    best_h = hr.copy()
                    best_n = int(best_n)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # ---------------- Phase 5: higher resolution, if useful --------------
    p5 = t0 + 0.90 * t_budget
    if best_n < 512 and time.time() < p5 - 120:
        n_hi = 512
        try:
            h_hi = resample_h(best_h, best_n, n_hi)
            c_hi = compute_c5(h_hi, n_hi)
            if np.isfinite(c_hi) and c_hi < best_c:
                best_h = h_hi.copy()
                best_c = float(c_hi)
                best_n = n_hi

            if n_hi % 2 == 0 and time.time() < p5 - 70:
                v_hi = to_complement_v(h_hi, n_hi)
                if v_hi is not None:
                    _, ch, hh = slsqp_builder_sequence(
                        v_hi, n_hi, 1,
                        alphas=[20.0, 200.0, 1000.0],
                        maxiter=130,
                        time_end=min(p5 - 45, time.time() + 90.0)
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(ch) and ch < best_c:
                        best_h = hh.copy()
                        best_c = float(ch)
                        best_n = n_hi

            if time.time() < p5 - 35:
                _, cg, hg = slsqp_builder_sequence(
                    h_hi, n_hi, 0,
                    alphas=[20.0, 200.0, 1000.0],
                    maxiter=90,
                    time_end=min(p5 - 15, time.time() + 70.0)
                )
                if np.isfinite(cg) and cg < best_c:
                    best_h = hg.copy()
                    best_c = float(cg)
                    best_n = n_hi

            if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < p5 - 25:
                hb, Mb = best_binary_search(best_h, best_n, p5 - 5, seed + 3333)
                if hb is not None and np.isfinite(Mb):
                    cbin = float(Mb) * (2.0 / best_n)
                    if cbin < best_c:
                        best_h = hb.astype(float).copy()
                        best_c = float(cbin)
                        best_n = int(best_n)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ---------------- Phase 6: final polish ------------------------------
    if time.time() < t_end - 35:
        alphas = [800.0, 3000.0, 8000.0]
        maxit = 100 if best_n <= 300 else 60
        try:
            _, cf, hf = slsqp_builder_sequence(
                best_h.copy(), best_n, 0,
                alphas=alphas,
                maxiter=maxit,
                time_end=t_end - 20
            )
            if np.isfinite(cf) and cf < best_c:
                best_h = hf.copy()
                best_c = float(cf)
                best_n = int(best_n)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < t_end - 18:
        try:
            vfin = to_complement_v(best_h, best_n)
            if vfin is not None:
                vc, cfch, hch = slsqp_builder_sequence(
                    vfin, best_n, 1,
                    alphas=[300.0, 2000.0],
                    maxiter=70,
                    time_end=t_end - 5
                )
                if np.isfinite(cfch) and cfch < best_c:
                    best_h = hch.copy()
                    best_c = float(cfch)
                    best_n = int(best_n)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Finalize feasibility and return.
    try:
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    except Exception:
        best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    if not np.isfinite(best_c):
        # Fall back to parent if something went wrong.
        best_h = parent_h.copy()
        best_n = parent_n
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    return main_run(seed=seed, budget_s=budget_s)
```
